This info-icon tooltip is the **lighter, non-destructive alternative** — it clarifies the same point (the total is CC revenue net of fees, checks excluded via the Check Client Rec'd mirror) **without touching the column name or the math**. Good compromise on the original concern.
- **For Todd**: add an `ⓘ` affordance by the Grand Total header with the tooltip copy above. **Implementation note**: this header is a **Syncfusion PivotView** column header, not a plain grid column, so the icon likely needs the pivot's header cell template / `cellTemplate` or a small `queryCellInfo`-style hook rather than a simple `<th>` edit — confirm the supported PivotView hook. Use the shared `info-tooltip` pattern / design-system tokens, WCAG-AA, focus-visible (keyboard-reachable, not hover-only). Exact hover string: *"CC Revenue less Fees. Total does NOT include Check Revenue."*
- **Severity**: UX (reporting clarity — CC-only total not obvious from the label)
- **RESOLUTION (Todd go, 2026-07-30, `d4115f55`)** — deviates from the literal ask, same intent: an **always-visible caption directly under the pivot** — *ⓘ Grand Total = CC Revenue less Fees. Total does NOT include Check Revenue.* — instead of an icon injected into the Syncfusion header (Todd ruled SF-internal DOM hooks out). Caption lives in OUR template, no hover/discovery needed, works on touch. Exact copy preserved.
- **Ann verify pass (07-31)** — ✅ **Caption content + placement GREAT** ("this is great"), but 🔴 **needs highlighting to stand out** — Ann: wrap it in an **amber "light" container**. Right now it reads as plain caption text; a tinted callout box makes the CC-only caveat pop. **For Todd:** wrap the caption in an **amber callout container** (design-system `--bs-warning` family — subtle amber background + a left accent border + the ⓘ icon), sized to stand out clearly. **⚠️ Apply the AM-019/042 lesson: make it genuinely prominent, not a faint `-subtle` tint** (Ann found those too weak); a proper amber callout with border/emphasis, like a real "note" box. Keep the exact copy.
- **Status**: 🟢 caption BUILT + content/placement ✅ accepted (Ann, 07-31); **🔴 RE-OPENED for styling** — wants an **amber highlighted callout container** (prominent, not subtle). 🟢 RESTYLED via AM-064 (Todd go, 2026-08-01, `58cffc55`) — caption now the shared `.tsic-callout--warning` amber callout (⚠ icon, full-width under the pivot). Copy unchanged. ✅ **VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 08-01)** via the AM-064 pass — amber Grand Total callout prominent; whole item closed.

### AM-055: [ARB Health → Defensive Email] Email substitution tokens render wrong — !PLAYER as "Last, First", !JOBNAME/!JOBLINK as Customer-only
- **Topic**: ARB Health → Behind in Payment → Send → Compose Defensive Email → the `!TOKEN` substitution variables in the sent/preview email
- **Source**: Ann's email review (2026-07-31) — sibling to PL-057 (same ARB defensive-email surface)
- **Where (verified)**: all three resolve in `ArbDefensiveService.ReplaceArbTokens` ([ArbDefensiveService.cs:633-644](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Admin/ArbDefensiveService.cs#L633)).
- **Three token defects:**
  1. **`!PLAYER` renders "Regan, Peyton" (Last, First) — should read "Peyton Regan" (First Last).** Line 635: `.Replace("!PLAYER", $"<strong>{reg.RegistrantName}</strong>")`. `reg.RegistrantName` is a **"Lastname, Firstname"** string (sort-order format) — fine for a grid column, **wrong for prose in an email** ("Dear Regan, Peyton" reads backwards). *For Todd:* render **First + Last** in natural order for `!PLAYER` (either carry first/last separately on the flagged-registrant projection and compose "First Last", or reverse the existing "Last, First" for this token). Confirm the projection source of `RegistrantName`.
  2. **`!JOBNAME` shows only the Customer name — should show "Customer:Job name".** Line 644: `.Replace("!JOBNAME", $"<strong>{reg.JobName}</strong>")`. Ann sees just the customer (e.g. "Top Threat Tournaments") not the full "Top Threat Tournaments:Fall Draw 2026". *For Todd:* confirm what `reg.JobName` is populated with on the ARB flagged-registrant projection — the DB `Jobs.JobName` is already the full **"Customer:Job"** string, so the token should render that full value (the projection appears to be dropping the job portion).
  3. **`!JOBLINK` shows the Customer name underlined (link works, lands correctly) — should show the URL or the full linked "Customer:Job name".** Line 643: `.Replace("!JOBLINK", $"<a href='https://www.teamsportsinfo.com/{reg.JobPath}' …>{HtmlEncode(reg.JobName)}</a>")`. The href is correct; the **anchor text** is the same customer-only `reg.JobName` as #2. *For Todd:* set the link text to the full **"Customer:Job name"** (fixing #2 fixes this too), or show the URL — Ann's preference is the linked full Customer:Job name.
- **For Todd (summary)**: fix `!PLAYER` name order (First Last), and make `!JOBNAME` / `!JOBLINK` carry the full **Customer:Job** name (trace the flagged-registrant projection that feeds `reg.RegistrantName` and `reg.JobName`). These are ARB-specific tokens (`ReplaceArbTokens`), separate from the full Search/Reg substitution engine — so the fix is local to this method + its projection.
- **ADDITIONS — cross-surface comparison with Search/Registrations email (Ann, 07-31):** the same tokens behave differently in the **Search/Registrations** batch email (canonical engine `TextSubstitutionService`) vs **ARB Health** (`ReplaceArbTokens`), so the two surfaces need reconciling:
  - **4. Token-name inconsistency — `!PERSON` (Search/Reg) vs `!PLAYER` (ARB Health).** The person token is **`!PERSON`** in the canonical engine (`tokens["!PERSON"] = f.Person` — [TextSubstitutionService.cs:430](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/TextSubstitution/TextSubstitutionService.cs#L430)) but **`!PLAYER`** in ARB Health ([ArbDefensiveService.cs:635](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Admin/ArbDefensiveService.cs#L635)). Same concept, two names → a director learns one and it fails on the other surface. **Consider consolidating** to a single token name across both (Ann's suggestion), or at least documenting/aliasing both. *(Note: `!PERSON` here reads in natural order already; ARB's `!PLAYER` is the "Last, First" one from item 1 — so consolidating also folds in the name-order fix.)* **Refined by AM-059 (07-31): prefer ALIASING over dropping one** — given the multi-role audience (Player / Club Rep / Staff), both `!PLAYER` and `!PERSON` can legitimately coexist as long as **both resolve to the registrant's natural-order First Last**. The goal is consistent *output*, not a single token name.
  - **5. `!JOBLINK` — display is RIGHT in Search/Reg but the link TARGET is wrong.** In Search/Reg, `!JOBLINK` correctly shows the full **"Customer:Job name"** as the anchor text (`f.JobName` is the full name) — better than ARB's customer-only display (item 3). **BUT** the link Ann clicked went to **"the last site I was in on TSIC," not the job's page.** Both engines hardcode the anchor as `href='https://www.teamsportsinfo.com/{JobPath}'` ([TextSubstitutionService.cs:451-452](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/TextSubstitution/TextSubstitutionService.cs#L451); ARB twin at [ArbDefensiveService.cs:643](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Admin/ArbDefensiveService.cs#L643)) — a **hardcoded PRODUCTION domain**, so off-production the link jumps to the live site (and/or lands on the last-visited site if `JobPath` doesn't resolve). *For Todd:* trace why the Search/Reg `!JOBLINK` lands on the wrong site while ARB's "goes to the right place" (item 3) despite identical construction — check the `JobPath` value on the Search/Reg projection and the hardcoded `teamsportsinfo.com` domain (should it be environment-aware?). **Best end-state = ARB's correct href + Search/Reg's correct "Customer:Job" display**, unified.
- **Severity**: UX / correctness (customer-facing email reads wrong — backwards names, job not identified, link mis-targets) + token-name inconsistency across two email surfaces
- **Cross-ref**: PL-057 (ARB defensive-email compose — plain-text body); same email, different aspect. **Consolidation candidate**: unify the ARB `ReplaceArbTokens` set with the canonical `TextSubstitutionService` token vocabulary (`!PERSON`/`!JOBNAME`/`!JOBLINK`) so both email surfaces share one token language.
- **Status**: ✅ **FIXED `df7049ec`** (2026-07-31) — items 1-3 done: `!PLAYER` renders natural order (First/Last carried on the projection, composed in `ProseName` — deliberately NOT parsing the comma, which breaks on suffixes); `!JOBNAME`/`!JOBLINK` now read `Jobs.JobName` (the full "Customer:Job") instead of `DisplayName`, matching the canonical engine. Item 4 addressed by aliasing **`!PERSON` to the same value** in `ReplaceArbTokens` — it previously passed through RAW into a customer email. **Item 5 (hardcoded `www.teamsportsinfo.com`) intentionally NOT changed**: www points at this app after cutover, so the literal is already correct in Production; making it environment-aware would put prod correctness at the mercy of a config value to fix a staging-only cosmetic. **Convergence onto `TextSubstitutionService` deliberately NOT attempted pre-go-live** — it rewrites a live send path to reclaim the `!OWEDNOW` occurrence/grace math. **OPEN: deploy + Ann E2E.**
- **Status**: 🟢 ITEMS 1–4 BUILT (Todd, 2026-07-31, `df7049ec` + regen `07c8abff`). ✅ **VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 07-31)** — `!PLAYER`/`!PERSON` render natural order ("Peyton Regan"); `!JOBNAME`/`!JOBLINK` carry the full Customer:Job. **Item 5 🟢 RESOLVED by `7bc94929`** (the 07-31 email-render modernization, landed after Ann's pass): the canonical engine's `!JOBLINK` (and `!JOBURL`/`!UNSUBSCRIBE`) now build from the environment-aware `_frontendBaseUrl` instead of the hardcoded production `www.teamsportsinfo.com` ([TextSubstitutionService.cs:451](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/TextSubstitution/TextSubstitutionService.cs#L451)) — off-prod links now stay on the sending environment, which is the "went to the last site I was in" symptom. **Ann verify post-deploy**: Search/Reg email with `!JOBLINK` from staging → link lands on the staging job page.

### AM-056: [Auth → Forgot Password] "Back to Sign In" link is a hardcoded absolute `/tsic/login` in BOTH cards — doesn't return to the originating job's login
- **Topic**: Forgot Password flow → the **"Back to Sign In"** link, which appears in **two places**: the Forgot Password form card, and the subsequent **"Check Your Email"** confirmation card.
- **Source**: Ann's email/auth review (2026-07-31)
- **Where (verified)** — [forgot-password.component.html](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/auth/forgot-password/forgot-password.component.html):
  - Card 1 (the form): `<a routerLink="/tsic/login" …>Back to Sign In</a>` ([:43](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/auth/forgot-password/forgot-password.component.html#L43)).
  - Card 2 ("Check Your Email"): `<a routerLink="/tsic/login" …>Back to Sign In</a>` ([:60](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/auth/forgot-password/forgot-password.component.html#L60)).
- **Root cause**: both links are **hardcoded absolute** `routerLink="/tsic/login"`. Real logins are nested under `:jobPath` → `/{jobPath}/login` ([app.routes.ts:48,59](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/app.routes.ts#L48)), but **forgot-password is a top-level route** (`/forgot-password`, [app.routes.ts:32](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/app.routes.ts#L32)) with no jobPath context, so it falls back to the generic **tsic** job's login. Result: a user who reached Forgot Password from a specific club's login (e.g. `/yellowjackets/login`) is sent to `/tsic/login` — the **wrong club's login**, losing their job branding/context. Also violates the project rule against absolute routerLinks (they don't preserve `:jobPath`).
- **For Todd**: make "Back to Sign In" return to the **originating job's** login in **both** cards. Since forgot-password sits outside the `:jobPath` shell, carry the source jobPath into the page (e.g. the login page's "Forgot password?" link passes `?jobPath=<path>` / `?returnUrl=`, and "Back to Sign In" routes to `/{jobPath}/login`; fall back to `/tsic/login` only when unknown). Fix **both** `:43` and `:60`.
- **⚠️ Same pattern elsewhere (fix together)**: **reset-password** has the identical hardcoded links — [reset-password.component.html:35](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/auth/reset-password/reset-password.component.html#L35) + [:103](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/auth/reset-password/reset-password.component.html#L103) and the post-reset redirect [reset-password.component.ts:60](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/auth/reset-password/reset-password.component.ts#L60) (`navigateByUrl('/tsic/login')`). The reset link arrives by email so it can carry the jobPath in the URL. Recommend fixing forgot-password + reset-password in one pass.
- **Severity**: UX / navigation (returns to the wrong/generic login, loses job branding; absolute-routerLink rule violation)
- **Cross-ref**: AM-020 / PL (password-reset flow).
- **Status**: ✅ **FIXED `264a5c34`** (2026-07-31) — all 5 hardcoded template links plus the post-reset `navigateByUrl` now build from a `loginLink` derived from `?jobPath=`, which the login page's "Forgot your password?" link supplies from the URL segment (falling back to the `?jobPath=` query form used by the embedded login). Falls back to `/tsic/login` when unknown, so the no-job case behaves exactly as before. **Residual**: the emailed reset URL does not yet carry a jobPath, so reset-password still falls back — the param is read, so it starts working the moment `AuthController` appends the job. That needs a job on the anonymous, username-only forgot-password request; deliberately not bundled here. **OPEN: deploy + Ann E2E.**
- **Status**: 🟢 BUILT (Todd, 2026-07-31, `264a5c34`) — both links now `[routerLink]="loginLink"` = `/{jobPath}/login` (jobPath via `?jobPath=` from login's "Forgot your password?" link; falls back to `/tsic/login`); reset-password fixed the same way. **🔴 RE-OPENED (Ann verify, 07-31) — "Back to Sign In" NOT WORKING in either card.** Ann's key point: even the **generic `/tsic/login` fallback should navigate and doesn't** — so this is NOT the jobPath logic, it's the **navigation itself failing at runtime**. Code reviewed and appears correct (login passes `?jobPath=` [login.component.ts:111-112], forgot reads it [forgot-password.component.ts:26], both cards bind `[routerLink]="loginLink"`), so the failure is runtime/build, not logic. **Ann (07-31): clicking does NOTHING AT ALL in both cards.** **⭐ Leading cause found:** the login route's guard `redirectAuthenticated: true` **bounces authenticated users away from `/login`** ([auth.guard.ts:100-124](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/infrastructure/guards/auth.guard.ts#L100): if isAuth → `toJob(user.jobPath)`/role-selection). **Ann is testing while logged in as admin**, so "Back to Sign In" → `/login` is intercepted and never lands on a login page → reads as dead. **But forgot-password is a logged-OUT flow** — a user who forgot their password isn't signed in — so the real-world test is **logged out**. **RETEST LOGGED OUT** (incognito / sign out): club login → "Forgot your password?" → "Back to Sign In" should return to that club's login. **(Ann tried a logout→login round-trip, still nothing — but that ends her LOGGED IN, so the bounce still applies. The valid test requires staying logged OUT at click time.)** **For Todd:** confirm the logged-out anchor navigates on both forgot-password + reset-password; if it still does nothing logged out, real bug (RouterModule *is* imported and `loginLink` is non-empty, so check the target route activating). Also confirm Ann has build `264a5c34`.
- **🟢 REAL BUG FOUND + FIXED (Todd go, 2026-08-01, `06fe444d`)** — the guard breaks the logged-OUT flow too: a logged-out hit on `/login` with no `force`/`returnUrl`/`intent` triggers the guard's **last-job convenience bounce** ([auth.guard.ts:108-109](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/infrastructure/guards/auth.guard.ts#L108)) → last-visited job's HOME, never a login page. Fix: all four links + the post-reset redirect carry **`force=1`** (on the guard's whitelist, read by nothing else). Logged-IN clicks still bounce to job home by design. ✅ **VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 08-01, logged out)** — "Back to Sign In" lands on the originating club's login from both cards; reset-flow links land on login too.

### AM-057: [Search/Registrations → Send Batch Email] Recipient list & Email Log don't show the parent (Contact 1/2) emails the message is actually sent to
- **Topic**: Search/Registrations → select players → **Send Batch Email** modal recipient list, and the resulting **Email Log / Email Detail / Sent-To** records
- **Source**: Ann's email review (2026-07-31) — selected 3 players; the modal showed one email each (one blank), raising: does it also email the parents (Contact 1 & 2)?
- **✅ VERIFIED IN CODE — send coverage is CORRECT (not a bug); this is a DISPLAY/LOGGING gap.** For a **Player**-role recipient, `BatchEmailRecipientFilter.ResolveRecipients` returns **mom + dad + the player's own email** (distinct, case-insensitive, blank/invalid stripped) — [BatchEmailRecipientFilter.cs:35-41](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/Email/BatchEmailRecipientFilter.cs#L35): `BuildSendableSet(new[] { family.MomEmail, family.DadEmail, ownEmail })`. Every other role → the registrant's own email. So the batch email **already goes to Contact 1 (mom) + Contact 2 (dad) + the player's own address** — Ann's "maybe it does and doesn't display?" is correct. (The blank in the modal, e.g. "Antenucci, Kaia", is the *player's own* email being empty on a child account — mom/dad still receive it.)
- **The gap (two places):**
  1. **Modal recipient list doesn't show the real send set.** The Send Batch Email modal displays only **one** address per registrant (the registrant's own email, from `GetRecipientEmailsByIdsAsync`, "registrant's own `User.Email`" — [IRegistrationRepository.cs:67-71](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.Contracts/Repositories/IRegistrationRepository.cs#L67)), not the mom+dad+own set that actually gets emailed. So a director can't see that the parents will be copied (and sees a blank for child accounts that will in fact be delivered to the parents).
  2. ~~**Email Log / Email Detail / "Sent To" must record every actual recipient address.**~~ **✅ CORRECTED 07-31 — ALREADY BUILT, no work.** The audit already stores every delivered address, not one per registrant: `SendWorkerAsync` enqueues **each** entry of `message.ToAddresses` ([EmailBatchService.cs:290](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/Email/EmailBatchService.cs#L290)), and `FlushAuditAsync` writes the whole queue, `;`-joined, into `EmailLogs.SendTo` ([:378](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/Email/EmailBatchService.cs#L378)). So mom + dad + player are each recorded. *(Unverified remainder: whether the Email Detail **view** renders the full `SendTo` string or truncates it — FE display only.)*
- **For Todd**: surface the **full resolved recipient set** (mom/dad/player, per `ResolveRecipients`) in the Send Batch Email modal so the director sees all addresses a message will hit — reuse `ResolveRecipients` for the preview so display == send. **That is the whole item** — the logging half is done.
- **Severity**: Transparency (director can't see that parents are copied) — send behavior AND the audit log are both already correct
- **Status**: ✅ **FIXED `1568fee7`** (2026-07-31) — resolved differently than proposed, per Todd: *why show an email at all?* The address column is gone. It could only ever show ONE of up to three addresses, and showed a BLANK where delivery succeeds — the reading Ann hit. The list now names the **people** selected (which is what the director actually picks; addresses are resolved at send from the family record) with a note that **player messages also reach the Contact 1 and Contact 2 addresses on file**. No endpoint, no DTO change, no extra query. Two latent bugs fixed in the same pass: the `@for` tracked by `r.email`, so two child accounts with blank emails collided on a duplicate track key; and names rendered as "Adragna , CeCe" from trailing whitespace in the stored last name. Logging half was already correct (`EmailLogs.SendTo`). **OPEN: deploy + Ann E2E.**
- **Status**: 🟢 BUILT (Todd, 2026-07-31, `1568fee7`) — modal drops the misleading address column, names the selected people, adds the Contact 1/2 note; logging half already built. ✅ **VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 07-31)** — list shows selected people, no misleading blank; the Contact 1/2 note is present and correct. **Only follow-up: the note's STYLING** (make it a highlighted icon callout) → folded into consolidated **AM-064** (shared prominent-callout treatment, 4th instance with AM-019/042/054). Functionally done.

### AM-058: ⚠️ LARGELY RETRACTED 07-31 — [Email substitution] Legacy tokens review — the "missing" content tokens EXIST under `!F-` names and ship on 1000+ live confirmation emails; the real delta is 2 tokens + a canned body
- **Topic**: Email substitution `!TOKEN` vocabulary — Legacy tokens vs the new engine (`TextSubstitutionService` / `TokenReplacer`)
- **Source**: Ann's email review (2026-07-31) — asked to review a Legacy token list for what didn't carry forward
- **✅ ALREADY PORTED (14 of Ann's ~20 — no action, exist in the new engine):** verified present in [TextSubstitutionService.cs](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/TextSubstitution/TextSubstitutionService.cs):
  - agegroup assignment → **`!AGEGROUPNAME`** · agegroup+team → **`!AGNPLUSTN`** · applied to category → **`!APPLIEDTOCATEGORY`** · club name → **`!CLUBNAME`** · jersey size → **`!JERSEY_SIZE`** · job description → **`!JOBDESCRIPTION`** · job logo → **`!JOBLOGO`** · league assignment → **`!LEAGUENAME`** · mailto (checks) → **`!MAILTO`** · payto (checks) → **`!PAYTO`** · registration Active/dropped → **`!REGISTRANT_ISACTIVEORDROPPED`** · role name → **`!ROLENAME`** · shorts size → **`!SHORTS_SIZE`** · team assignment → **`!TEAMNAME`**. *(Also present: `!JOBNAME`, `!JOBCODE`, `!JOBPATH`, `!JOBURL`, `!PERSON`, `!SEASON`, `!SPORT`, `!UNIFORM_NO`, `!TSHIRT_SIZE`, `!TRAININGGROUP`, `!VOLPOSITION`, `!AMTFEES/PAID/OWED`, `!SUBSCRIPTIONID/STATUS`, `!USLAXVALIDTHROUGHDATE`, invite/unsubscribe tokens.)*
- **⚠️ RETRACTION (07-31) — the "6 genuinely missing content tokens" finding was WRONG.** It came from grepping guessed token names (`!…REFUNDPOLICY`, `!…WAIVER`). The tokens exist under `!F-` names, all wired in [TextSubstitutionService.cs:591-606](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/TextSubstitution/TextSubstitutionService.cs#L591):

  | Ann's item | Actual token | Live use (of 1065 job templates) |
  |---|---|---|
  | ADMIN: player refund policy | `!F-REFUND-PLAYER-WAIVER` | 0 |
  | Player Waiver | `!F-WAIVER-PLAYER` | **1025** |
  | Player Code of Conduct | `!F-COC-WAIVER-PLAYER` | 0 |
  | Player Covid Waiver | `!F-COVID-WAIVER-PLAYER` | 17 |
  | Staff Waiver | `!F-WAIVER-ADULT` | **596** |
  | Staff — Tournament Registered Teams | *(none — genuinely absent)* | — |

  Counts are from `Jobs.Jobs.PlayerReg_ConfirmationEmail` / `AdultReg_ConfirmationEmail` on the dev DB. **These tokens are not dormant — they render on every confirmation email today.** (`!F-TEAMS` = 354 templates.) The two zero-use tokens are still wired and null-safe; the COC zero is explained below.
- **🔴 TRUE legacy→new delta = 2 tokens.** Diffed the full vocabularies (new engine vs [legacy ITextSubstitutionService](../../reference/TSIC-Unify-2024/TSIC-Unify-Services/ITextSubstitutionService.cs)): only **`!F-STAFFTOURNAMENT-ASSIGNMENTS`** (Ann's staff-tournament registered teams) and **`!S`** are absent. Everything else ported.
- **⚠️ RETRACTION — "Waitlist Activation email" is NOT an email flow, and never was in Legacy either.** It is the last entry in Legacy's *substitution-variable dropdown* ([SearchController.cs:152](../../reference/TSIC-Unify-2024/TSIC-Unify/Controllers/Search/SearchController.cs#L152)); selecting it just calls `ckEditor.setData(<fixed HTML>)` ([Index.cshtml:1216-1231](../../reference/TSIC-Unify-2024/TSIC-Unify/Views/Search/Index.cshtml#L1216)) — a **canned body** the director then sends by hand from the batch email. No template record, no trigger, no automatic send anywhere in Legacy. The canned body is built entirely from tokens that **all exist** in the new engine: `!PERSON !TEAMNAME !JOBNAME !AMTOWED !JOBURL !FAMILYUSERNAME`. So "port the template + activation trigger" would be building something that never existed. What Ann actually loses is the **one-click canned body**.
- **🔎 Why directors think the tokens are missing:** **zero** `!F-*` references exist anywhere in the frontend (grepped all `.ts`/`.html`). Both composers hardcode their own short, player-centric chip lists, so the whole content-block family is invisible in the UI even though the engine resolves it. **That is AM-059's problem** — this item collapses into it.
- **For Todd (revised)**: (1) **no token porting needed** — expose the existing vocabulary via AM-059's shared catalog; (2) add a **canned-template mode** to the picker (entries *replace* the body instead of inserting a token), with Waitlist Activation as entry one; (3) optionally add `!F-STAFFTOURNAMENT-ASSIGNMENTS` — the only real backend gap.
- **⚠️ Two traps when exposing these:**
  - Legacy's dropdown emitted **`!!F-COC-WAIVER-PLAYER`** (double bang) — a Legacy bug, which is why 0 templates use it. Don't copy the typo forward.
  - `BuildWaiverHtmlAsync` selects from a `dynamic` anonymous object carrying only **5** waiver properties. `AdultRegRefundPolicy` and `AdultRegCodeOfConduct` are columns but are **not** in that object — adding tokens for them without extending it throws `RuntimeBinderException` at send time.
- **Severity**: (mostly retracted — Legacy parity is essentially complete; the gap is UI discoverability, owned by AM-059)
- **Cross-ref**: **AM-059 (this item collapses into it)**, AM-055 (ARB token naming), PL-025/050/051 (waitlist surfacing).
- **Status**: ⚠️ **LARGELY RETRACTED 2026-07-31** — original finding was grep-based and wrong; corrected against code + DB counts. Residual work: canned-template mode (small) + `!F-STAFFTOURNAMENT-ASSIGNMENTS` (optional). Back to Todd.

### AM-059: [Search/Registrations] Player Details fly-in email compose ≠ the Search/Registrations table email — unify tokens + Draft-with-AI
- **Topic**: The two email-compose surfaces under Search/Registrations — the **Player Details fly-in** (per-player) vs the **table batch email** (multi-select) — should be the same display + function.
- **Source**: Ann's email review (2026-07-31)
- **Two asks:**
  1. **Insert Token parity** — the fly-in offers a **different token set** than the batch email. They should match.
  2. **Draft-with-AI parity** — the fly-in's "Draft with AI" should **mirror** the batch modal's Compose Email AI display/function. Net: **the two surfaces should be the same.**
- **✅ VERIFIED — the token sets are hardcoded separately and differ:**
  - **Fly-in** — hardcoded chips in the template ([registration-detail-panel.component.html:651-661](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/registration-detail-panel.component.html#L651)): `!PERSON !EMAIL !JOBNAME !AMTFEES !AMTPAID !AMTOWED` always, plus `!FAMILYUSERNAME !JOBLINK !SUBSCRIPTIONID !SUBSCRIPTIONSTATUS` conditionally.
  - **Batch modal** — the `BASE_TOKENS` array ([batch-email-modal.component.ts:13-24](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/batch-email-modal.component.ts#L13)): `!PERSON !EMAIL !FAMILYUSERNAME !JOBNAME !JOBLINK !AMTFEES !AMTPAID !AMTOWED !SEASON !SPORT !CUSTOMERNAME` + `!USLAXVALIDTHROUGHDATE` + seeded invite tokens.
  - **Delta**: the fly-in is **missing `!SEASON`, `!SPORT`, `!CUSTOMERNAME`, `!USLAXVALIDTHROUGHDATE`**; it uniquely carries `!SUBSCRIPTIONID` / `!SUBSCRIPTIONSTATUS` (legitimately useful for a per-player ARB context). So they've drifted because each hardcodes its own list.
  - Both surfaces do have a "Draft with AI" control ([registration-detail-panel.component.html:621](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/registration-detail-panel.component.html#L621); batch modal too), but as **separate implementations** — hence the display/behavior differences Ann sees.
- **⚠️ ALL ROLES, not just Player (Ann, 07-31)**: the same compose-email surface is used for **every role** — **Club Rep and Staff see the same display** as Player (the fly-in `registration-detail-panel` is role-agnostic; its base token chips render for all roles). So the unification and the token-availability review must cover **Player, Club Rep, and Staff** — not just the player case. **Consider role-appropriate token availability given the wider audience**: a Club Rep email may want club/team-oriented tokens; a Staff email its own; the current shared list is player-centric. The right design is likely a **common base set + role-conditional additions** (as the fly-in already does for family/ARB), applied consistently across both compose surfaces and all roles.
- **Note — `!PLAYER` AND `!PERSON` may BOTH make sense (Ann, 07-31)**: given the multi-role audience, keeping **both** tokens can be legitimate **even if both resolve to the registrant's First Last name**. `!PERSON` reads naturally for a generic registrant (Club Rep / Staff / Player); `!PLAYER` reads naturally when the audience is specifically players. **This nuances AM-055's "consolidate to one name"** → prefer **aliasing** (both tokens resolve to the same natural-order First-Last value) over dropping one. Update AM-055 accordingly: the goal is *consistent output* (natural order, both surfaces), not necessarily a single token name.
- **For Todd**: unify the two compose surfaces **for all roles**. (1) **Single token source** — the fly-in should offer the same catalog as the batch modal (add `!SEASON`/`!SPORT`/`!CUSTOMERNAME`/`!USLAXVALIDTHROUGHDATE`); best is to hoist `BASE_TOKENS` into a **shared constant/component** both consume, keeping the ARB `!SUBSCRIPTIONID`/`!SUBSCRIPTIONSTATUS` extras available where relevant, and **adding role-conditional tokens** for Club Rep / Staff. (2) **Shared Draft-with-AI** — same prompt UI, same behavior/copy on both. (3) **Both `!PLAYER` and `!PERSON`** resolve to the registrant's natural-order First Last (alias, not de-dup). Ideally extract **one email-compose component** used by both the fly-in and the batch modal, role-aware, so they can't drift again.
- **✅ ASK 1 (token parity) — FIXED 2026-07-31.** The two hardcoded lists are gone. A single catalog now lives in [email-templates.ts](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/email-templates.ts) — `EMAIL_BASE_TOKENS` (11, typed `EmailTokenInfo` with label + description), plus `USLAX_VALID_THROUGH_TOKEN` and `SUBSCRIPTION_TOKENS` as conditional add-ons. **Both** surfaces consume it: the fly-in ([registration-detail-panel.component.ts:1010](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/registration-detail-panel.component.ts#L1010)) appends `SUBSCRIPTION_TOKENS` for ARB registrants; the batch modal ([batch-email-modal.component.ts:149](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/batch-email-modal.component.ts#L149)) appends the USLax token when the job validates memberships. The fly-in's missing `!SEASON`/`!SPORT`/`!CUSTOMERNAME` came with it. **They can no longer drift** — that was the point of hoisting rather than copying.
- **✅ ASK 3 (`!PLAYER` / `!PERSON` alias) — FIXED** (AM-055 work): both resolve to the registrant's natural-order First Last in the ARB resolver, and `TextSubstitutionService` already aliased them.
- **⏸️ ASK 2 (shared Draft-with-AI) — PARKED, deliberately.** The **behavior is already identical**: both `draftWithAi()` implementations call the same `registrationSearch.aiComposeEmail(prompt)` → the same `POST /ai-compose/email` → the same `AiComposeService`. What differs is only **markup/styling** (the fly-in uses `.ai-compose-group`, the modal a `.starter-card` with a `.btn-ai`). Extracting one shared email-compose component is a **multi-hundred-line refactor of two live, load-bearing surfaces** for a cosmetic delta — that is the wrong risk to take this close to go-live, and the drift risk it would guard against is already closed by the shared token catalog. Revisit post-cutover if Ann still finds the two screens jarring.
- **Severity**: (largely resolved — token parity + aliasing shipped; only presentational unification deferred)
- **Cross-ref**: AM-055 (token naming/consolidation — this note nuances it toward aliasing), AM-057 (batch-email recipients), PL-057 (ARB compose).
- **Status**: ✅ **FIXED (asks 1 + 3) 2026-07-31** — shared token catalog shipped, both surfaces consume it. **Ask 2 (one extracted compose component) PARKED post-cutover** — AI behavior is already shared; the remaining delta is styling only. **OPEN: staging deploy → Ann E2E.**
- **Cross-ref**: AM-055 (token naming/consolidation — this note nuances it toward aliasing), AM-057 (batch-email recipients), PL-057 (ARB compose).
- **pt1 BUILT (Todd, 2026-07-31, `815d4d6a`)** — shared `email-templates.ts` catalog; the fly-in gains `!SEASON`/`!SPORT`/`!CUSTOMERNAME`. `!PLAYER`/`!PERSON` aliasing done in AM-055.
- **Ann verify pass (07-31) — 🔴 pt1 RE-OPENED, two token-gating bugs in the fly-in (code-confirmed):**
  1. **`!USLAXVALIDTHROUGHDATE` still MISSING from the fly-in.** The batch modal adds it when the JOB uses USA Lacrosse validation (`jobFlags()?.usLaxMembershipValidated` — [batch-email-modal.component.ts:150](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/batch-email-modal.component.ts#L150)); the fly-in's `availableTokens()` never adds it (not even imported — [registration-detail-panel.component.ts:20,1008-1011](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/registration-detail-panel.component.ts#L1008)). *Fix:* add `USLAX_VALID_THROUGH_TOKEN` to the fly-in gated on the same job flag.
  2. **`!SUBSCRIPTIONID`/`!SUBSCRIPTIONSTATUS` gated wrong — Ann wants them for ALL registrants on ARB sites.** Fly-in adds them only when the *individual* registrant `detail()?.hasSubscription` ([:1009](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/registration-detail-panel.component.ts#L1009)) — so PIF/non-subscriber registrants on an ARB site see nothing; and the **batch modal doesn't offer them at all** (base + USLax only). *Fix:* gate on the **job-level ARB flag** (`adnARB`), not per-registrant subscription, and offer them on **both** surfaces so an ARB-site director can use them for any registrant.
- **Severity**: UX / consistency (fly-in token set still not at parity; ARB-site tokens under-exposed)
- **Status**: 🔴 pt1 RE-OPENED (Ann, 07-31) → 🟢 BOTH GATING BUGS FIXED (Todd go, 2026-08-01, `d5924664`) — one job-level rule on BOTH surfaces (base + `!USLAXVALIDTHROUGHDATE` on USLax jobs + subscription tokens on ARB jobs; per-registrant `hasSubscription` gating removed). ✅ **VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 08-01)** — ARB job offers subscription tokens for PIF registrants + batch modal; USLax job offers `!USLAXVALIDTHROUGHDATE`; plain job = base only, both surfaces. **Still open: pt2 (shared Draft-with-AI, PARKED post-cutover) + all-roles review.**

### AM-060: 🟡 FIXED (Todd, 07-31) — awaiting Ann verify post-deploy — [Search/Registrations → Registrant fly-in] "Re-Send Confirmation Email" (blue), on the Accounting tab
- **Topic**: Registrant Details fly-in (`registration-detail-panel`) — add an admin action to **resend the registrant's confirmation email**.
- **Source**: Ann's email review (2026-07-31)
- **Request (Ann)**: Add a **"Re-Send Confirmation Email"** badge/button in the registrant fly-in, placed **near the "Change Job" badge**, styled in **blue**. (Exact label spelling per Ann: **"Re-Send Confirmation Email"** — hyphenated.)
- **Placement**: next to the **Change Job** control ([registration-detail-panel.component.html:280-284](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/registration-detail-panel.component.html#L280)). *(Note: Change Job is gated player-only + SuperUser/SuperDirector; Re-Send Confirmation should NOT be player-gated — every role has a confirmation email, so it should show for Player / Club Rep / Staff / Adult. Confirm the admin role gate Ann wants — likely Director-and-above.)*
- **➕ ALSO consider a placement under the Payment Ledger (Ann, 07-31)**: since an admin **updating a payment does NOT auto-notify the registrant** (verified — no auto-email on corrections/fee adjustments; the confirmation only fires on the registrant's own payment events), put the **Re-Send Confirmation Email** action **on/under the Payment Ledger** (the fly-in Accounting tab) with an inline **note to the admin**: *"If you've updated payment, select Re-Send Confirmation Email to notify the registrant."* This puts the resend exactly where an admin just changed money and closes the notify-the-registrant gap at the point of action. Todd's call whether it lives in **both** spots (near Change Job **and** on the Payment Ledger) or just the Ledger — the Ledger placement is the more purposeful one given the workflow.
- **✅ Backend already exists (verified) — resend endpoints per role:**
  - Player → `POST confirmation/resend` ([PlayerRegistrationConfirmationController.cs:91](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Controllers/PlayerRegistrationConfirmationController.cs#L91)).
  - Adult → `POST confirmation/{registrationId}/resend` ([AdultRegistrationController.cs:324](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Controllers/AdultRegistrationController.cs#L324) → `SendConfirmationEmailAsync`).
  - Team → `SendConfirmationEmail` ([TeamRegistrationController.cs:730](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Controllers/TeamRegistrationController.cs#L730)).
  - **⚠️ Caveat for Todd**: the Player/Adult resend endpoints read as **self-service / authenticated to the caller** (the Player one logs "user identity not found" and keys off the caller). An **admin resending for an arbitrary registrant** may need those endpoints opened to an admin role (registrationId-scoped) or a small admin-scoped resend wrapper. Confirm before wiring the button.
- **For Todd**: add a **blue** "Re-Send Confirmation Email" action beside Change Job in the fly-in; wire it to the correct **per-role** resend endpoint for the open registrant; show a success/failure toast reporting what happened (the backend already "reports what actually happened"). Use the design-system **`--bs-primary`** (blue) treatment — consistent with Ann's repeated colored-badge asks (AM-042, AM-047) and her blue preference (AM-019); WCAG-AA, focus-visible.
- **Severity**: UX / feature (no admin resend-confirmation affordance today; useful when a registrant didn't get / lost their confirmation)
- **Cross-ref**: AM-042 / AM-047 (colored-badge treatment), AM-019 (blue preference), AM-018 (confirmation email CC/BCC), AM-062 (wizard test-send), PL-061 (payment-triggered send — NOT affected by the copies change below).
- **✅ BUILT (Todd, 07-31, `a76debdc`)** — placement went to the **Accounting tab** (Ann's Payment-Ledger preference won over "near Change Job": the motivating case is a payment edit that notifies nobody, so the action sits where the money was just changed). Blue `btn-outline-primary`, exact hyphenated label, shows for **every role**. **ONE button, two behaviors (Todd, 07-31)**: off-prod the button IS the test-send popover (opens the anntsic@gmail.com swap, renders the registrant's confirmation, delivers to the test inbox — the real send couldn't deliver off-prod anyway); on Production it does the real resend. No separate "Send Test Email" button.
  - **New admin-scoped endpoints** (the ⚠️ caveat above was real — the self-service endpoints were NOT opened to admins): `POST registration-search/{id}/resend-confirmation` + `/test-send-confirmation`, AdminOnly + job-scoped like every other fly-in action. Role-routing is server-side: **Player → the FAMILY confirmation** (one email covering every sibling in the job, to mom + dad + family player emails — there is deliberately no per-player variant; the toast discloses this), **Club Rep → team pipeline mailing the registration's owner** (the self-service path mails the *caller* — it would have mailed the admin), everyone else → adult pipeline. Always forces past the `BConfirmationSent` latch.
  - **Redelivery copy semantics (Todd decision, 07-31)**: a **resend keeps the job's Reply-To but does NOT re-send the job's CC/BCC copies** — the copies audience got the original. Applies to this admin action **and** the three wizard Re-Send buttons. New confirmation events (initial send, PL-061 payment receipts) still carry copies.
  - **Security fix found en route**: the adult resend/test-send endpoints had **no ownership check** — any authenticated user could fire them for any registrationId (test-send = cross-job content disclosure, sandbox-only). Now owner-only, matching the team path.
- **07-31 SU-only rule (Todd)**: every test-email affordance on a shared environment is **Superuser-only** — the fly-in popover plus the batch-email test-send, the invite test-inbox field, ARB Health, and USLax test-sends. Directors on staging see the real controls (which render but never deliver off-prod). Wizard popovers stay dev-only regardless of role — the wizard session carries the registrant's role, so role-gating is structurally impossible there, and dev.\* wizard screens face real customers.
- **Ann verify (post staging deploy — log in as your SUPERUSER account, or the popover won't show)**:
  1. Fly-in → **Accounting tab** → one blue **Re-Send Confirmation Email** button. On staging, clicking it opens the **test popover** (defaulted to anntsic@gmail.com) — it never emails the registrant.
  2. Fire it on a **player**, an **adult (coach/staff/referee)**, and a **club rep** → your inbox gets the right per-role confirmation, subject stamped `[TEST — rendered for: …]` (player says "this family").
  3. The **real resend** (toast reporting family scope/recipient email, no-email-on-file warning) is **Production-only behavior** — spot-check it at cutover along with Reply-To.
- **Status**: 🟢 FIXED (Todd, 07-31, `a76debdc`) — ✅ **VERIFIED PASSING via test emails (Ann, 08-01)** — blue Re-Send Confirmation button on the Accounting tab; test popover delivers the correct per-role confirmation to the test inbox (player/adult/club rep, `[TEST — rendered for: …]`, player = "this family"). **OPEN — Production-only, spot-check at cutover:** the REAL resend delivery + Reply-To (deferred to go-live, like AM-018/020).

### AM-061: ✅ CLOSED — behavior accepted (Ann, 07-31) — [Search/Teams] No general/compose email from Search/Teams; only the ARB-autopay-failure reminder + the club rep's login "Pay Balance Due" quicklink
- **Topic**: Search/Teams → emailing club reps of teams that owe a balance
- **Source**: Ann's testing (2026-07-31) — on **Lax For The Cure: Summer 2027** (748 active teams, **70 owing $36,330**, non-ARB) she found **no email options** in Search/Teams.
- **✅ VERIFIED — the ONE email control in Search/Teams is ARB-autopay-failure-only:**
  - The only email affordance is the **"Send Invoice Reminders"** button, rendered **`@if (flaggedCount() > 0)`** ([search-teams.component.html:363-372](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/teams/search-teams.component.html#L363)), where `flaggedCount` = results with **`paymentFlagged`** ([search-teams.component.ts:155-156](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/teams/search-teams.component.ts#L155)).
  - **`PaymentFlagged` rule** ([TeamRepository.cs:1184-1186](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.Infrastructure/Repositories/TeamRepository.cs#L1184)): `has an ADN subscription **AND** OwedTotal > 0 **AND** (ADN status not "active" **OR** an NSF reversal)` — i.e. **a team on ARB autopay whose autopay FAILED and still owes.** Not "a team that owes."
  - **LFTC Summer 2027 is `adnARB=0` (non-ARB)** → no team has an ADN subscription → nothing can ever flag → the button **never shows**, despite 70 teams owing. (Confirmed the same for other non-ARB tournaments, e.g. American Select Lacrosse:Main Event 2025.)
- **The gap (Ann)**: On a **non-ARB (check/CC) tournament**, a director has **no way to email owing club reps** from Search/Teams — no "remind teams that owe," no general compose-email to selected teams (only per-row `mailto:` links). And the club rep's **only** prompt to pay is **reactive**: a **"Pay Balance Due" quicklink that appears only when the club rep logs in** — there is no proactive email nudge. So a $36K-in-arrears tournament has no email path to collect.
- **For Todd**: give Search/Teams a **payment-reminder email path that works for non-ARB tournaments** — either (a) broaden the reminder to **any owing team** (not just ARB-autopay-failed), and/or (b) add a **general compose-email to selected teams/club reps** on Search/Teams (parity with the Search/Registrations batch email, AM-057/AM-059). Decide the "who's flagged" definition: today it's ARB-failure-specific; Directors of check/CC tournaments need "owes a balance" too. (Optionally: an automatic/queued balance-due reminder to owing club reps, complementing the login quicklink.)
- **✅ DISPOSITION (Ann, 07-31): behavior is CORRECT — NO emails from Search/Teams. No change wanted.** Ann reviewed the finding and accepts the current design: Search/Teams intentionally has no general/compose email; club reps are prompted to pay via the **"Pay Balance Due" quicklink on login**, and the only Search/Teams email is the ARB-autopay-failure invoice reminder. Kept on record (not deleted) so the investigation + decision aren't re-raised. The "for Todd" broadening ideas below are **NOT being pursued.**
- **Severity**: (closed — accepted as designed; documented only)
- **Cross-ref**: AM-057 / AM-059 (Search/Reg email surfaces), PL-037/052 (Club Rep Pay Balance Due quicklink).
- **Status**: ✅ CLOSED — behavior accepted, no change (Ann, 2026-07-31). Original code-verified finding retained for the record.

### AM-062: [Email testing] Wire the "Test Email" (test-send) affordance into Invite-to-register emails and Re-Send Confirmation emails
- **Topic**: A **"Test Email" badge** so Ann can test an email flow by sending the rendered message to a test inbox (Sandbox) instead of real recipients.
- **Source**: Ann's email review (2026-07-31)
- **Request (Ann)**: Todd to **link up a Test Email badge** for testing: **(1) Invite-to-register emails** and **(2) Re-Send Confirmation emails**.
- **✅ Plumbing already exists (verified) — this is a WIRING job, not new infra:**
  - Shared **`test-send-button`** component ([shared-ui/components/test-send-button/test-send-button.component.ts](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/shared-ui/components/test-send-button/test-send-button.component.ts)) — already used in **ARB Health**, the **Search/Registrations batch-email modal**, and the **registrant fly-in** email.
  - Backed by **`EmailTestSendService`** ([Services/Shared/Email/EmailTestSendService.cs](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/Email/EmailTestSendService.cs)) — **Sandbox-only**: sends an already-rendered subject/body to a single test inbox, subject stamped as a test; **refuses in Production** ("Test sends are not permitted in Production").
- **What's missing / for Todd:**
  1. **Invite-to-register emails** — the batch-email modal has the Test Email button, but the **invite** send is a distinct seeded flow (`!INVITE_LINK` / `!CLUBREP_INVITE_LINK` / `!INVITE_EXPIRES` resolved **server-side per recipient**). Wire Test Email so a test send of an invite **renders the invite content with a representative (sample or real signed) link/expiry** to the test inbox — so Ann can see exactly what an invitee receives. Confirm the invite tokens resolve in the test path (they won't if the test send bypasses the per-recipient invite resolution).
  2. **Re-Send Confirmation emails** — this rides **AM-060** (the new Re-Send Confirmation action, not yet built). Add a Test Email path that **renders the registrant's confirmation template** (per role) and routes it to the test inbox instead of the registrant, so Ann can verify confirmation content/formatting without emailing the family. (Confirmations render server-side via `PlayerRegConfirmationService` / Adult / Team services — the test path renders + redirects the recipient.)
- **For Todd**: reuse the existing `test-send-button` + `EmailTestSendService` (Sandbox-only) and wire both flows to it. Keep the Production guard. Note: because `EmailTestSendService` is Sandbox-only, this is for **pre-go-live dev/staging testing** — it complements, not replaces, the go-live delivery checks.
- **✅ BOTH HALVES RESOLVED 2026-07-31:**
  1. **Invite half — already worked, no change needed.** In Staging the batch-email modal renders a **"To (test inbox)"** field in invite mode; the invite send goes to that address with the **real per-recipient invite tokens resolved** (the send path is the normal one, only the delivery address is swapped). The generic Test Email button is deliberately **hidden** in invite mode because the preview/render path does *not* resolve invite tokens — showing it there would produce a misleading test. So Ann already has a faithful invite test; she just needs to be told the field is the mechanism.
  2. **Confirmation half — BUILT** (`80f4e7a8` backend, `84e0ef7a` frontend). New `POST .../test-send` endpoints for **Player** (`PlayerRegistrationConfirmationController`), **Adult** (`AdultRegistrationController`), and **Team** (`TeamRegistrationController`); each renders that registrant's real confirmation via the same code path the live send uses (extracted `RenderConfirmationAsync` / `BuildConfirmationPreviewAsync`) and hands it to `EmailTestSendService`. All three **hard-refuse when `IsLiveProduction()`**. Frontend: the shared `test-send-button` popover now sits beside "Re-Send Confirmation Email" on the **team review step**, **adult confirmation step**, and **player confirmation step**, non-prod only, defaulting to **anntsic@gmail.com**.
  - **Did NOT require AM-060.** The test-send hangs off the **wizard confirmation screens** (where the registrant/club rep already is), so it did not have to wait for the admin fly-in resend action. AM-060 remains open on its own merits.
- **⚠️ SCOPE OF THE TEST BUTTON — read before using it as proof (Ann)**: the Test Email popover proves the confirmation **renders** correctly — tokens, amounts, teams, the eCheck banner, formatting in a real inbox. It renders through the *same* code the live send uses, so content sign-off here is genuine. It does **NOT** prove the email is ever **sent** in production flows: it's a separate entry point that never touches the payment code. **Anything about whether a confirmation actually fires still needs a real transaction** — specifically **PL-061** (club rep paid a balance due and got no email; fixed, never exercised against a real charge). Use the popover for *"does it look right"*, a real payment for *"does it arrive"*.
- **Severity**: (resolved — testability restored on both flows)
- **Cross-ref**: **PL-061 (club-rep confirmation not sent — the send-side counterpart to this render-side tool)**, AM-060 (admin fly-in resend — still open, independent), AM-059 (batch-email compose), AM-058 (token/email flows), go-live email checklist.
- **Invite-half re-open detail**: confirmation half FIXED (`80f4e7a8` + `84e0ef7a`, verified via AM-060 08-01); **🔴 "To (test inbox)" field doesn't appear in the invite email popup (player OR club rep), so Ann can't test invites.** Root cause (code): the invite test-inbox field is gated **`@if (isStaging && isSuperuser())`** ([batch-email-modal.component.html:164](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/batch-email-modal.component.html#L164)) where `isStaging = envName === 'staging'` ([.ts:77](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/batch-email-modal.component.ts#L77)) — **staging-build-only**, unlike every other test affordance which uses the broader **`isNonProd`** (dev + staging, [.ts:81](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/batch-email-modal.component.ts#L81); the generic test button at [:291](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/batch-email-modal.component.html#L291) uses it). So on dev/localhost the field is hidden while the other test buttons show. **⚠️ CONFIRMED WORSE (Ann, 08-01): NOT visible on localhost OR the staging build** — so this is **deeper than the `isStaging` gate** (on the staging build `isStaging` should be `true`, and Ann is SuperUser — she saw the AM-060 fly-in popover, so `isSuperuser()` resolves elsewhere). Static analysis says the field *should* render on staging-as-SU, so **Todd must runtime-debug**. Suspects, in order: (1) `isStaging` isn't actually `true` on the staging build — verify `environment.envName === 'staging'` after the angular.json `fileReplacements` for the staging config (a wrong/missing replacement would leave `envName` off 'staging'); (2) `isSuperuser()` (`auth.isSuperuser`) isn't truthy in *this* component's injector context even though it is in the fly-in; (3) a parent `@if` (invite-inputs fieldset / target-event guard at [:132-162](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/search/registrations/components/batch-email-modal.component.html#L132)) collapses the group before it renders. **For Todd:** find why the block doesn't render on staging-as-SU, then **also broaden the gate to `isNonProd`** so it works on dev too (align with the other test affordances). Applies to **both** invite modes (`player` + `clubrep`).
- **Status**: ✅ **VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 08-01) — BOTH HALVES.** Confirmation test-send verified via AM-060; **invite test-inbox field found + tested working** — the earlier "not visible" was a locate issue, not a bug (the field renders on the staging build for SuperUser as designed). Invite test emails deliver to the "To (test inbox)" address with real resolved invite tokens for both player + club-rep invites. *(Note: the `isStaging`-vs-`isNonProd` gate inconsistency still means the invite field won't show on plain dev/localhost — leave as-is unless Todd wants dev parity; not blocking.)*
- **07-31 design correction (Todd, rode AM-060)**: the three wizard confirmation screens no longer show TWO buttons (Re-Send + Send Test Email). **One button**: in dev the Re-Send button IS the test popover (anntsic swap — the real send can't deliver off-prod anyway); on Staging/Production it's the real resend. Wizard popover gate stays `!environment.production` (dev-only) on purpose: wizard screens face **real customers on dev.\*** (SEDONA is client-facing), who must never see the test popover — unlike the admin-only fly-in, which shows it on Staging too.

### AM-063: [Email → Draft with AI] ✨ ENHANCEMENT — let the AI pull forward prior email copy from the Email Log ("reuse last year's thank-you email")
- **Topic**: Draft-with-AI in the email composer — retrieve/adapt a prior sent email from the Email Log by natural-language request.
- **Source**: Ann (2026-07-31). Example ask: *"Can you pull up my email thanking registrants for their participation in the tournament from last year's Email Log?"* — bringing forward the text copy as a starting draft.
- **✅ Feasible — the raw material exists (verified):**
  - The **Email Log stores the full body**, not just metadata: `Jobs.emailLogs` = `JobID`, `subject (nvarchar max)`, **`msg (nvarchar max)` — the body**, `sendTS`, `sendTo`, `sendFrom`, `senderUserID`. So prior sent copy is retrievable by job / subject / date / content.
  - The **Draft-with-AI engine already exists**: `AiComposeService.ComposeEmailAsync(prompt, jobName, season)` ([Services/Shared/AiCompose/AiComposeService.cs:46](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/backend/TSIC.API/Services/Shared/AiCompose/AiComposeService.cs#L46)) — today it composes fresh from a prompt; this extends it to **retrieve-then-adapt**.
- **The feature**: a natural-language request → search the Email Log for the best-matching prior email (over `subject` + `msg`, filtered by date/"last year") → **load its text into the compose body** as a starting draft (optionally let the AI freshen it for the current event). Turns institutional email copy into reusable templates without hunting.
- **⚠️ Nuance — "last year's" is cross-JOB**: the log is keyed by `JobID`, and last year's tournament is a **different job** (same customer, prior season). So retrieval must span the **customer's prior jobs** (e.g. resolve "the tournament from last year" → the prior-year job for this customer, then search its `emailLogs`). Confirm the customer→prior-jobs lookup.
- **Suggested path for Todd**: (1) **MVP without AI first** — a **searchable Email Log** (by subject/keyword/date, across the customer's jobs) with a **"Reuse / copy into a new email"** action. That alone delivers most of the value. (2) **AI layer on top** — interpret the natural-language description ("thanking registrants for participation") and pick/rank the best-matching prior email semantically, then seed the draft. Reuses `AiComposeService`.
- **Severity**: ✨ Enhancement / future (not go-live) — high delight, not a bug. Depends on AM-057's Email-Log work (that item already touches log storage/display).
- **Cross-ref**: AM-057 (Email Log/Detail — recipient accuracy + log surfacing), AM-059 (AI compose), AM-043/045 (bulletin AI). 
- **Status**: Open — filed 2026-07-31 (feasibility confirmed: log stores body in `msg`). Enhancement backlog — Todd/Ann to prioritize post-go-live.

### AM-064: [Design system] 🎯 CONSOLIDATED — one prominent "info callout" treatment for ALL informational notes (Ann's recurring "highlight this" feedback)
- **Topic**: A single, reusable, **visually prominent** info/note callout used everywhere the app shows an informational note — replacing the muted plain-text / `-subtle`-tint treatments Ann keeps flagging as too faint.
- **Source**: Recurring feedback across the 07-30/07-31 verify passes — Ann has now asked **four+ times** for the same thing: make informational notes stand out.
- **The pattern (why this exists):** every time an informational note ships as muted text or a faint `bg-*-subtle` tint, Ann can't see it and re-opens the item:
  - **AM-019** — "0 = unlimited" note (`bg-info-subtle` pill) → too subtle, wants bolder blue.
  - **AM-042** — bulletin save-hint (`bg-info-subtle` pill) → too subtle, wants bolder blue.
  - **AM-054** — Grand Total caption (plain text) → wants an amber highlighted container.
  - **AM-057** — batch-email Contact 1/2 note (plain text) → wants an icon/highlighted callout.
  - *(Contrast: **AM-047** back-badge used a primary tint + border + hover-deepen and Ann said "looks great" — that weight is the target.)*
- **For Todd — build ONE shared callout component/mixin and reuse it:** an **icon + tinted container + accent border + emphasis text**, genuinely prominent (NOT `-subtle`; borrow the AM-047 weight). Support the semantic variants Ann uses — **blue/info** (informational, e.g. "0 = unlimited", save-hint), **amber/warning** (caveats, e.g. Grand Total "CC-only"). Design-system tokens, light/dark aware, WCAG AA, reduced-motion clean. Then **retrofit AM-019, AM-042, AM-054, AM-057 to use it** (closes all four in one pass) and adopt it as the default for future notes.
- **Severity**: UX / design-system consistency — a systemic fix that closes 4 open re-opens and prevents future ones.
- **Cross-ref**: AM-019, AM-042, AM-054, AM-057 (all fold into this), AM-047 (the treatment that passed — use its weight).
- **Status**: 🟢 BUILT (Todd go, 2026-08-01, `58cffc55` + `a515aa8f`) — shared `.tsic-callout` class family (global `_callouts.scss`), AM-047 weight (1px border + 4px left accent + 600 text + solid-semantic icon), `--info`/`--warning` variants + `--block` modifier, light/dark + WCAG AA. ✅ **VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 08-01)** — all four retrofitted spots confirmed prominent: AM-019 (LADT 0=unlimited, blue), AM-042 (bulletin save-hint, blue), AM-054 (Grand Total, amber + ⚠), AM-057 (Contact 1/2, icon callout). Adopted as the default for future notes — no more bare `-subtle` chips / muted footnotes.

### AM-065: [Configure / Job Settings] Confirmation text boxes should always be editable (not gated on registration-active) + relabel the Teams tab "Club Reps/Teams"
- **Source**: Ann (2026-08-02), reviewing Job Settings text sections
- **Part 1 — Confirmation copy should be editable ANYTIME, not dependent on whether registration is currently Active/QuickLink-enabled.**
  - **What Ann saw** (screenshot): the **Club Rep Confirmations** section shows *"Team registration is not offered on this job — enable it above to use this copy."* when team registration isn't enabled. The copy reads as unavailable/blocked.
  - **Why it matters (Ann)**: a Director may want to **edit the confirmation text before releasing registration**, or at times other than when registrations are occurring. The confirmation/copy text boxes should be **visible and editable all the time**, independent of the registration-availability toggle.
  - **Where (code)**: the sections are gated on the registration-availability flags — Club Rep Confirmations on the Teams tab ([teams-tab.component.html:75-78](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/configure/job/tabs/teams-tab.component.html#L75): `@if (!bRegistrationAllowTeam())` → closed-note); the same pattern gates **Coach / Referee / College-recruiter** copy on the Coaches tab ([coaches-tab.component.html:114/154/193](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/configure/job/tabs/coaches-tab.component.html#L114)) and the **Player** confirmation section on the Player tab (`bRegistrationAllowPlayer` gating). *(The RTE boxes DO render when you expand the accordion, but the collapsed "not offered — enable it above" note signals the copy is unavailable and discourages editing.)*
  - **For Todd**: make the confirmation/copy text sections **always editable regardless of the registration-availability toggle** — drop (or reword) the `section-closed-note` gating so a Director can prep the copy anytime. If a "this won't be sent until registration is enabled" hint is still wanted, phrase it as informational (reuse the AM-064 callout) **without hiding or disabling the editor**. Apply consistently across **Club Rep (Teams), Player, and Coach/Ref/Recruiter (Coaches)** copy sections. Confirm nothing about *saving* the copy depends on the toggle (it shouldn't — the columns persist regardless).
- **Part 2 — Relabel the Teams tab "Club Reps/Teams"** *(placement of the boxes is great, Ann's only request)*. Since the tab holds **Club Rep** settings/confirmations (not just teams), users won't think to look under "Teams" for Club Rep info — labeling it **"Club Reps/Teams"** (Legacy-like) fixes discoverability. Ann: there's **plenty of room in the header space**.
  - **⚠️ THIRD time this rename is requested** — declined in the **ConfigureMenus sweep** ("won't change, leave 'Teams'") and again in **AM-036** (Ann acknowledged the decline). Ann is re-raising with the discoverability rationale (part 1's context makes it sharper: Club Rep *confirmations* live here). **For Todd + Ann to settle directly** given the repeat — not a silent re-decline. If approved: rename the tab label to "Club Reps/Teams" (label only; no route/data change).
- **Severity**: UX (config editing blocked by an unrelated toggle) + tab-label discoverability (re-raise)
- **Cross-ref**: AM-036 (tab-rename declined twice — this is the 3rd ask), AM-064 (callout for any retained "not sent yet" hint), AM-013/035 (the Roster-Visibility "where do I find this" discoverability theme).
- **Status**: 🟢 **Part 1 BUILT** (Todd go, 2026-08-02, `0c3f0f40`) — wording fix, no behavior change. **Audit correction:** nothing was ever gated. The confirmation RTEs sit in accordions whose *default open state* is a `linkedSignal` seeded from the availability toggle ([teams-tab.component.ts:38](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/configure/job/tabs/teams-tab.component.ts#L38), [coaches-tab.component.ts:42-44](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/configure/job/tabs/coaches-tab.component.ts#L42)); the `section-closed-note` renders **only while collapsed**. Expand and the editors are live and save normally regardless of the toggle — the copy columns persist independently, as Ann suspected. Also note the **Player tab copy was already ungated and always-expanded** ([player-tab.component.html:94](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/configure/job/tabs/player-tab.component.html#L94)), so it needed no change. Todd's call: **fix with words, keep the behavior** — all five notes (Club Rep/Teams; Coach, Referee, Recruiter, Adult Waivers/Coaches) now read *"… is not offered on this job right now. You can still open this section and edit the copy — it will be used when you enable it …"*, replacing the dead-end "enable it above to use this copy." **Ann verify post-deploy**: on a job with team registration off, Job Settings → Teams → the collapsed Club Rep Confirmations note invites editing; expand → both RTEs edit and **Save persists**; same on Coaches for coach/referee/recruiter/waivers. **Part 2 BUILT** (Todd go, 2026-08-02, `2f8d79b0`) — settled after two prior declines. Tab label **"Teams" → "Teams/ClubReps"** ([job-config.component.ts:57](../../TSIC-Core-Angular/src/frontend/tsic-app/src/app/views/configure/job/job-config.component.ts#L57)). Label only — no route, tab key, or data change.
  - **Why this wording** rather than Ann's "Club Reps/Teams": the tab strip runs 10 tabs on deliberately terse labels — "Comms" (not Communications), "Adult" (not Coaches & Staff), "Mobile/Store" — which is what drove the two earlier declines. "Teams/ClubReps" spells out Club Reps for discoverability while keeping the slash-compound form "Mobile/Store" already established, and leads with Teams so the tab keeps its existing identity. *(Todd landed on this after first trying the shorter "Teams/Reps" — "Reps" alone was too clipped for a Director scanning for Club Reps.)*

Terse strip + fuller toast is the existing pattern, so nothing else needed changing.
  - **Ann verify post-deploy**: Configure → Job Settings → the tab reads **Teams/ClubReps**; saving it still toasts "Teams & Club Reps saved."; strip does not wrap or crowd at typical widths (check a narrow window too — this is now the longest label in the strip).

Closing it means dropping the toggle→collapse linkage (sections default open, like Player). Deferred with Part 1 scoped to wording only; re-file if Ann hits it.
- **RESOLUTION (Todd, 2026-08-02)** — Part 1: visibility toggles now editable on their tabs + confirmation sections collapse-by-flow but expandable (`7b60d6d7`); role editors show effective `?? AdultReg` value (`28ccf7d2`); Club Rep Confirmations moved to the Teams tab (`38ab4d62`); Adult Policies/Waivers moved to end (`52715891`).
- **Ann verify pass (08-02):**

- **Status**: 🟡 **Part 2 ✅ VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 2026-08-05)** — tab reads **"Teams/ClubReps"** (Ann accepts Todd's ordering over her "Club Reps/Teams"). **Part 1 🔴 STILL RE-OPENED (Ann re-tested 2026-08-05)** — the always-editable fix **did NOT land for Club Rep or Adult Confirmations**: they remain gated on the QuickLink / registration-availability toggle. **Ann's requirement is unambiguous: the Club Rep AND all Adult confirmation/waiver text boxes must be OPEN and editable ALL THE TIME, not dependent on the QuickLink toggle** (same as Player already is).
